    <hr class="section-divider" />

    <div class="options-card">
      <h3>Opciones del Sistema</h3>

      <label class="switchWrap">
        <input type="checkbox" id="chkOverlay">
        <span class="switch"></span>
        <span class="switchText">Mostrar esqueleto</span>
      </label>

      <div class="mt-14">
        <label class="threshold-label">
          <span>Sensibilidad alerta</span>
          <input type="range" id="rngThrOn" min="0.10" max="0.95" step="0.01" value="0.65">
          <span id="lblThrOn" class="threshold-value">0.65</span>
        </label>
      </div>
    </div>

    <h2>Alertas</h2>
    <div id="alerts" class="alerts"></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="right">
    <div class="flex-between gap-10">
      <h2 class="mt-0">Monitoreo</h2>
      <label class="switchWrap toolbar-switch" title="Silenciar sonido de alertas">
        <input type="checkbox" id="chkMuteAlert">
        <span class="switch"></span>
        <span class="switchText">Silenciar sonido</span>
      </label>
    </div>

    <div id="camGrid" class="grid mt-10"></div>
  </section>
</main>

<footer>
  <p>Proyecto de tesis enfocado en la deteccion de acciones anomalas en video</p>
  <p>Propiedad de: Pedro Retuerto Diaz</p>
</footer>

<script>
const alertQueue = [];
let showingCam = null;
let switching = false;

const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const tokenKey = 'sicher.token';
const emailKey = 'sicher.email';
const alertMuteKey = 'sicher.alert_muted';
const wsMap = new Map();
const wsReconnectTimers = new Map();
const wsReconnectAttempts = new Map();
const wsExpectedClose = new Set();
const wsLastStatus = new Map();
const wsLastStatusAt = new Map();
const cameraMeta = new Map();
let activeBigCam = null;

const apiUrl = path => (window.sicherApiUrl ? window.sicherApiUrl(path) : path);
const inferenceUrl = path => (window.sicherInferenceUrl ? window.sicherInferenceUrl(path) : path);
const wsUrl = path => (window.sicherWsUrl ? window.sicherWsUrl(path) : path);

const toDomKey = value => String(value || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
const domId = (prefix, serialNumber) => `${prefix}-${toDomKey(serialNumber)}`;
const CONN_STATUS_LABEL = {
  online: 'Online',
  offline: 'Offline',
  error: 'Error'
};

function authHeaders() {
  const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
  const email = localStorage.getItem(emailKey);
  const userId = localStorage.getItem('sicher.user_id');
  if (email) headers['X-User-Email'] = email;
  if (userId) headers['X-User-Id'] = userId;
  return headers;
}

function enqueueCamera(serialNumber, pvid) {
  if (!alertQueue.some(c => c.serial_number === serialNumber)) {
    alertQueue.push({ serial_number: serialNumber, pvid });
  }
}

function setConnectionState(serialNumber, status, detail = '') {
  const dot = document.getElementById(domId('dot', serialNumber));
  const rowStatus = document.getElementById(domId('conn', serialNumber));
  const cardStatus = document.getElementById(domId('connCard', serialNumber));
  const label = CONN_STATUS_LABEL[status] || status;

  if (dot) {
    dot.classList.remove('st-online', 'st-offline', 'st-error');
    dot.classList.add(`st-${status}`);
  }
  if (rowStatus) {
    rowStatus.textContent = label;
    rowStatus.classList.remove('st-online', 'st-offline', 'st-error');
    rowStatus.classList.add(`st-${status}`);
    rowStatus.title = detail || '';
  }
  if (cardStatus) {
    cardStatus.textContent = label;
    cardStatus.classList.remove('st-online', 'st-offline', 'st-error');
    cardStatus.classList.add(`st-${status}`);
    cardStatus.title = detail || '';
  }
}

async function reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, status, detail = '', force = false) {
  const now = Date.now();
  const lastStatus = wsLastStatus.get(serialNumber);
  const lastAt = wsLastStatusAt.get(serialNumber) || 0;
  if (!force && lastStatus === status && (now - lastAt) < 20000) return;

  wsLastStatus.set(serialNumber, status);
  wsLastStatusAt.set(serialNumber, now);

  try {
    await fetch(apiUrl(`/cameras/${encodeURIComponent(serialNumber)}/status`), {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: authHeaders(),
      body: JSON.stringify({ status, detail: detail || null })
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn(`No se pudo registrar estado ${status} para ${serialNumber}:`, e);
  }
}

function clearReconnectTimer(serialNumber) {
  const timer = wsReconnectTimers.get(serialNumber);
  if (timer) {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    wsReconnectTimers.delete(serialNumber);
  }
}

function scheduleReconnect(serialNumber, src, locationDescription = '') {
  if (wsExpectedClose.has(serialNumber)) return;
  if (wsReconnectTimers.has(serialNumber)) return;

  const attempts = wsReconnectAttempts.get(serialNumber) || 0;
  const delayMs = Math.min(30000, 1000 * (2 ** attempts));
  const detail = `reintento ${attempts + 1} en ${Math.round(delayMs / 1000)}s`;
  setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'error', detail);
  reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, 'error', detail).catch(() => {});

  const timer = setTimeout(() => {
    wsReconnectTimers.delete(serialNumber);
    wsReconnectAttempts.set(serialNumber, attempts + 1);
    openWS(serialNumber, src, locationDescription, { isReconnect: true });
  }, delayMs);
  wsReconnectTimers.set(serialNumber, timer);
}

async function processQueue() {
  if (switching) return;
  if (alertQueue.length === 0) return;

  switching = true;
  alertQueue.sort((a, b) => b.pvid - a.pvid);

  const next = alertQueue.shift();
  showingCam = next.serial_number;

  highlightCamera(showingCam);
  await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 5000));

  restoreCamera(showingCam);
  showingCam = null;
  switching = false;
  processQueue();
}

function highlightCamera(serialNumber) {
  const grid = $('#camGrid');
  const card = document.getElementById(domId('card', serialNumber));
  if (!card) return;

  if (activeBigCam && activeBigCam !== serialNumber) {
    const prev = document.getElementById(domId('card', activeBigCam));
    if (prev) prev.classList.remove('bigCam');
  }

  activeBigCam = serialNumber;
  grid.prepend(card);
  card.classList.add('bigCam');
  grid.classList.add('smallLayout');
}

function restoreCamera(serialNumber) {
  const card = document.getElementById(domId('card', serialNumber));
  if (!card) return;

  card.classList.remove('bigCam');

  if (activeBigCam === serialNumber) {
    activeBigCam = null;
    $('#camGrid').classList.remove('smallLayout');
  }
}

/* ===========================
   OVERLAY: CARGAR ESTADO
=========================== */
async function loadOverlay() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(inferenceUrl('/overlay/get'));
    const data = await res.json();
    $('#chkOverlay').checked = data.overlay === true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('No se pudo cargar overlay:', e);
  }
}

/* ===========================
   OVERLAY: CAMBIAR ESTADO
=========================== */
async function setOverlay() {
  const val = $('#chkOverlay').checked;
  await fetch(inferenceUrl('/overlay/set'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ overlay: val })
  });
}

$('#chkOverlay').addEventListener('change', setOverlay);

/* ===========================
   THRESHOLD: CARGAR Y GUARDAR
=========================== */
const THR_HYST_GAP = 0.15;

async function loadThreshold() {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(inferenceUrl('/threshold/get'));
    const data = await res.json();
    const thrOn = (data.thr_on_override ?? data.thr_on_model ?? 0.65);
    $('#rngThrOn').value = thrOn;
    $('#lblThrOn').textContent = Number(thrOn).toFixed(2);
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('No se pudo cargar threshold:', e);
  }
}

let thrSaveTimer = null;
async function setThreshold() {
  const thrOn = parseFloat($('#rngThrOn').value);
  const thrOff = Math.max(0, thrOn - THR_HYST_GAP);
  $('#lblThrOn').textContent = thrOn.toFixed(2);
  clearTimeout(thrSaveTimer);
  thrSaveTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
    try {
      await fetch(inferenceUrl('/threshold/set'), {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ thr_on: thrOn, thr_off: thrOff })
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('No se pudo guardar threshold:', e);
    }
  }, 200);
}

$('#rngThrOn').addEventListener('input', setThreshold);

function loadAlertSoundPreference() {
  const muted = localStorage.getItem(alertMuteKey) === '1';
  const chkMute = $('#chkMuteAlert');
  if (chkMute) chkMute.checked = muted;
}

function setAlertSoundPreference() {
  const muted = $('#chkMuteAlert')?.checked === true;
  localStorage.setItem(alertMuteKey, muted ? '1' : '0');
  if (muted) pendingAlertSound = false;
}

/* ===========================
    SESION
=========================== */
function setWho() {
  const nombre = localStorage.getItem('sicher.nombre');
  const apellido = localStorage.getItem('sicher.apellido');
  const role = localStorage.getItem('sicher.role') || localStorage.getItem('sicher.charge') || '-';

  if (nombre && apellido) {
    $('#who').innerHTML = `Bienvenido: <b>${nombre} ${apellido}</b> `;
  } else {
    $('#who').innerHTML = 'No autenticado';
  }
}

function logout() {
  localStorage.removeItem(tokenKey);
  localStorage.removeItem(emailKey);
  localStorage.removeItem('sicher.nombre');
  localStorage.removeItem('sicher.apellido');
  localStorage.removeItem('sicher.role');
  localStorage.removeItem('sicher.charge');
  localStorage.removeItem('sicher.user_id');
  window.location.href = '/';
}

/* ===========================
   CAMERAS CRUD
=========================== */
async function addCamera() {
  const serial_number = $('#cam_serial').value.trim();
  const src = $('#cam_src').value.trim();
  const location_description = $('#cam_location').value.trim();

  if (!serial_number || !src) return alert('Completa serial y fuente');

  const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/cameras'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(),
    body: JSON.stringify({ serial_number, src, location_description })
  });

  if (!res.ok) {
    const err = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    alert(err.error || 'No se pudo registrar la camara');
    return;
  }

  $('#cam_serial').value = '';
  $('#cam_src').value = '';
  $('#cam_location').value = '';

  await loadCameras();
}

async function deleteCamera(serialNumber,btnElement) {
  closeWS(serialNumber, { removeCard: true, report: false });
  if (btnElement) {
    const row = btnElement.closest('.cam-row');
    if (row) row.remove();
  }
  const headers = {};
  const email = localStorage.getItem(emailKey);
  const userId = localStorage.getItem('sicher.user_id');
  if (email) headers['X-User-Email'] = email;
  if (userId) headers['X-User-Id'] = userId;

  try {
    await fetch(apiUrl('/cameras/' + encodeURIComponent(serialNumber)), {
      method: 'DELETE',
      headers
    });
  
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error al borrar en el servidor", error);
    alert("Hubo un error de conexión. Recargando cámaras...");
    await loadCameras(); 
  }

  await loadCameras();
}

function rowCam(cfg) {
  const serial = cfg.serial_number || cfg.cam_id;
  const location = cfg.location_description || 'Sin ubicacion';
  const div = document.createElement('div');
  div.className = 'cam-row';
  div.innerHTML = `
    <div class="flex-row gap-8 mb-8">
      <div class="statusdot" id="${domId('dot', serial)}"></div>
      <div class="flex-1">
        <b>${serial}</b>
        <div class="muted">${location}</div>
        <div id="${domId('conn', serial)}" class="cam-conn st-offline">Offline</div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="cam-actions">
      <button data-serial="${serial}" data-src="${encodeURIComponent(cfg.src)}" data-loc="${encodeURIComponent(location)}" class="btnOpen cam-btn cam-btn-open">Abrir</button>
      <button data-serial="${serial}" class="btnClose cam-btn cam-btn-close">Cerrar</button>
      <button data-serial="${serial}" class="btnDel cam-btn cam-btn-del">Eliminar</button>
    </div>
  `;
  return div;
}

async function loadCameras() {
  const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/cameras'), { headers: authHeaders() });
  const cams = await res.json();

  cameraMeta.clear();

  const list = $('#camList');
  list.innerHTML = '';

  cams.forEach(cfg => {
    const serial = cfg.serial_number || cfg.cam_id;
    if (!serial) return;
    cameraMeta.set(serial, cfg);
    list.appendChild(rowCam(cfg));
    if (wsMap.has(serial)) {
      setConnectionState(serial, 'online');
    } else if (wsLastStatus.has(serial)) {
      setConnectionState(serial, wsLastStatus.get(serial) || 'offline');
    } else {
      setConnectionState(serial, 'offline');
    }
  });

  list.querySelectorAll('.btnOpen').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => openWS(
      b.dataset.serial,
      decodeURIComponent(b.dataset.src || ''),
      decodeURIComponent(b.dataset.loc || '')
    );
  });
  list.querySelectorAll('.btnClose').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = () => closeWS(b.dataset.serial);
  });
  list.querySelectorAll('.btnDel').forEach(b => {
    b.onclick = (e) => deleteCamera(b.dataset.serial, e.target);
  });
}

/* ===========================
    WEBSOCKET STREAM
=========================== */
function camCard(serialNumber, locationDescription = '') {
  const key = toDomKey(serialNumber);
  const meta = cameraMeta.get(serialNumber);
  const src = meta ? meta.src : '';
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'camcard';
  card.id = `card-${key}`;
  card.innerHTML = `
    <div class="camhead">
      <div>
        <h3>${serialNumber}</h3>
        <div class="cammeta">${locationDescription || 'Sin ubicacion'} · <span id="meta-${key}">p_win: 0.000 · p_vid: 0.000</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="frame">
      <img id="img-${key}" />
    </div>
    <div class="stats">
      <div>TS: <span id="ts-${key}" class="muted">-</span></div>
      <div>Amenaza: <b id="state-${key}" class="ok">OK</b></div>
      <div>Conexion: <b id="${domId('connCard', serialNumber)}" class="st-offline">Offline</b></div>
      <div>Prob: <b id="prob-${key}" class="muted">0.000</b></div>
    </div>
    <div class="cam-src-label">${src}</div>
  `;
  return card;
}

function openWS(serialNumber, src, locationDescription = '', opts = {}) {
  const { isReconnect = false } = opts;
  if (!serialNumber) return;

  const cardId = domId('card', serialNumber);
  if (!document.getElementById(cardId)) {
    $('#camGrid').appendChild(camCard(serialNumber, locationDescription));
  }

  if (wsMap.has(serialNumber)) return;
  clearReconnectTimer(serialNumber);
  if (!isReconnect) {
    wsExpectedClose.delete(serialNumber);
    wsReconnectAttempts.set(serialNumber, 0);
  }
  setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'offline', 'Conectando...');

  const qs = src ? `?src=${encodeURIComponent(src)}` : '';
  const url = wsUrl(`/ws/stream/${encodeURIComponent(serialNumber)}${qs}`);
  const ws = new WebSocket(url);

  ws.onopen = () => {
    const srv = $('#srv');
    if (srv) srv.textContent = url;
    wsReconnectAttempts.set(serialNumber, 0);
    setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'online');
    reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, 'online', isReconnect ? 'reconectado' : 'conexion abierta', isReconnect).catch(() => {});
  };

  ws.onclose = (ev) => {
    wsMap.delete(serialNumber);
    if (wsExpectedClose.has(serialNumber)) {
      wsExpectedClose.delete(serialNumber);
      clearReconnectTimer(serialNumber);
      return;
    }
    setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'offline', ev?.reason || '');
    reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, 'offline', ev?.reason || '').catch(() => {});
    scheduleReconnect(serialNumber, src, locationDescription);
  };

  ws.onerror = () => {
    setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'error', 'Fallo de conexion');
    reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, 'error', 'fallo websocket').catch(() => {});
  };

  ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
    try {
      const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data);

      if (msg.type === 'frame') {
        const msgSerial = msg.serial_number || msg.cam_id || serialNumber;
        const key = toDomKey(msgSerial);

        const img = document.getElementById(`img-${key}`);
        const prob = document.getElementById(`prob-${key}`);
        const ts = document.getElementById(`ts-${key}`);
        const meta = document.getElementById(`meta-${key}`);
        const st = document.getElementById(`state-${key}`);

        if (img) img.src = 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + msg.jpg_b64;
        if (prob) prob.textContent = Number(msg.p_vid || 0).toFixed(3);
        if (ts) ts.textContent = new Date(Number(msg.ts || 0) * 1000).toLocaleTimeString();
        if (meta) {
          meta.textContent = `p_win: ${Number(msg.p_win || 0).toFixed(3)} · p_vid: ${Number(msg.p_vid || 0).toFixed(3)}`;
        }

        if (st) {
          if (msg.on) {
            st.textContent = 'ALERTA';
            st.className = 'alert';
            enqueueCamera(msgSerial, Number(msg.p_vid || 0));
            processQueue();
          } else {
            st.textContent = 'OK';
            st.className = 'ok';
          }
        }
      } else if (msg.type === 'alert') {
        addAlert(msg);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  };

  wsMap.set(serialNumber, ws);
}

function closeWS(serialNumber, options = {}) {
  const { removeCard = true, report = true } = options;
  wsExpectedClose.add(serialNumber);
  clearReconnectTimer(serialNumber);
  wsReconnectAttempts.set(serialNumber, 0);

  const ws = wsMap.get(serialNumber);
  if (ws) {
    ws.close();
    wsMap.delete(serialNumber);
  }

  setConnectionState(serialNumber, 'offline', 'Cerrado por usuario');
  if (report) {
    reportCameraStatus(serialNumber, 'offline', 'cerrado por usuario').catch(() => {});
  }
  if (removeCard) {
    const card = document.getElementById(domId('card', serialNumber));
    if (card) card.remove();
  }
}

async function persistPendingAlert(msg) {
  const serial_number = msg.serial_number || msg.cam_id;
  const prob = Number(msg.prob || 0);
  const ts = Number(msg.ts || (Date.now() / 1000));

  if (!serial_number) return;

  const payload = {
    serial_number,
    prob,
    timestamp: ts,
    status: 'pending'
  };

  await fetch(apiUrl('/alerts/save'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(),
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });
}

async function acceptAlert(ev) {
  const btn = ev.currentTarget;
  const serial_number = btn.dataset.serial;
  const prob = parseFloat(btn.dataset.prob || '0');
  const ts = parseFloat(btn.dataset.ts || '0');

  const payload = {
    serial_number,
    prob,
    timestamp: ts,
    status: 'true_positive',
    reviewer_email: localStorage.getItem(emailKey) || null
  };

  await fetch(apiUrl('/alerts/save'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(),
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  btn.closest('.alertrow')?.remove();
}

async function rejectAlert(ev) {
  const btn = ev.currentTarget;
  const serial_number = btn.dataset.serial;
  const prob = parseFloat(btn.dataset.prob || '0');
  const ts = parseFloat(btn.dataset.ts || '0');

  const payload = {
    serial_number,
    prob,
    timestamp: ts,
    status: 'false_positive',
    reviewer_email: localStorage.getItem(emailKey) || null
  };

  await fetch(apiUrl('/alerts/save'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: authHeaders(),
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  btn.closest('.alertrow')?.remove();
}

/* ===========================
   SONIDO DE ALERTA
=========================== */
let alertAudioCtx = null;
let pendingAlertSound = false;
let audioUnlockBound = false;

function getAlertAudioCtx() {
  if (alertAudioCtx) return alertAudioCtx;
  const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
  if (!AudioCtx) return null;
  alertAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
  return alertAudioCtx;
}

async function unlockAlertAudio() {
  const ctx = getAlertAudioCtx();
  if (!ctx) return false;

  if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {
    try {
      await ctx.resume();
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('No se pudo reanudar el audio de alertas:', e);
    }
  }

  if (ctx.state === 'running') {
    if (audioUnlockBound) {
      ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart'].forEach(eventName => {
        document.removeEventListener(eventName, unlockAlertAudio);
      });
      audioUnlockBound = false;
    }

    if (pendingAlertSound) {
      pendingAlertSound = false;
      playAlertSound();
    }
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function bindAlertAudioUnlock() {
  if (audioUnlockBound) return;
  ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart'].forEach(eventName => {
    document.addEventListener(eventName, unlockAlertAudio, { passive: true });
  });
  audioUnlockBound = true;
}

function playAlertSound() {
  if ($('#chkMuteAlert')?.checked === true) {
    pendingAlertSound = false;
    return;
  }

  const ctx = getAlertAudioCtx();
  if (!ctx) return;

  if (ctx.state !== 'running') {
    pendingAlertSound = true;
    unlockAlertAudio();
    return;
  }

  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc.type = 'sine';
  osc.frequency.value = 880;
  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, ctx.currentTime);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
  osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
  osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.5);
}

function addAlert(msg) {
  const serial_number = msg.serial_number || msg.cam_id;
  if (!serial_number) return;

  playAlertSound();

  const ts = Number(msg.ts || (Date.now() / 1000));
  const prob = Number(msg.prob || 0);

  persistPendingAlert({ serial_number, prob, ts }).catch(e => {
    console.warn('No se pudo persistir alerta pendiente:', e);
  });

  const row = document.createElement('div');
  row.className = 'alertrow';

  const tsText = new Date(ts * 1000).toLocaleString();

  row.innerHTML = `
    <div class="alert-info">
      <b>${serial_number}</b>
      <div class="muted">prob=${prob.toFixed(3)} · ${tsText}</div>
    </div>
    <button class="sec action-btn btnOk" data-serial="${serial_number}" data-prob="${prob}" data-ts="${ts}">Aceptar</button>
    <button class="sec action-btn btnNo" data-serial="${serial_number}" data-prob="${prob}" data-ts="${ts}">Rechazar</button>
  `;

  $('#alerts').prepend(row);

  row.querySelector('.btnOk').onclick = acceptAlert;
  row.querySelector('.btnNo').onclick = rejectAlert;
}

/* ===========================
    INIT
=========================== */
$('#btnLogout').onclick = logout;
$('#btnAdd').onclick = addCamera;
$('#chkMuteAlert').addEventListener('change', setAlertSoundPreference);
$('#btnToggleReg').onclick = () => {
  const sec = $('#regSection');
  const btn = $('#btnToggleReg');
  sec.classList.toggle('hidden');
  btn.classList.toggle('collapsed');
};

(async function init() {
  if (!localStorage.getItem(tokenKey)) {
    window.location.href = '/';
    return;
  }

  loadAlertSoundPreference();
  bindAlertAudioUnlock();
  setWho();
  await loadOverlay();
  await loadThreshold();
  await loadCameras();
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

:root {
  --bg: #0b0f17;

<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/>
<title>Sistema de Deteccion</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/login.css"/>
<script src="/config.js"></script>
</head>
<body>
<div class="wrap">
  <div class="shell">
    <div class="logo">
      <div class="badge">🛡️</div>
      <h1>Tesis de Deteccion</h1>
    </div>

    <h2>Iniciar sesion</h2>
    <div id="err" class="err"></div>
    <div id="tries" class="mut" style="display:none"></div>

    <label>Correo</label>
    <input id="email" class="in" placeholder="usuario@vigilia.io" autocomplete="username"/>

    <label>Contrasena</label>
    <input id="password" type="password" class="in" placeholder="********" autocomplete="current-password"/>

    <div class="row">
      <label class="mut remember-label">
        <input id="remember" type="checkbox"/> Recuerdame
      </label>
      <div class="mut">
        <a href="#" onclick="alert('Pide a un jefe restablecer tu clave.');return false;">¿Olvido su contrasena?</a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="mt-14">
      <button id="btn" class="btn">Iniciar sesion</button>
    </div>

    <div class="foot">
      <a href="#" onclick="toggleRegistro();return false;">Crear cuenta</a>
    </div>

    <div id="regBox">
      <h2>Crear cuenta</h2>

      <div id="regErr" class="err"></div>

      <label>Nombre</label>
      <input id="reg_nombre" class="in" placeholder="Juan"/>

      <label>Apellido</label>
      <input id="reg_apellido" class="in" placeholder="Perez"/>

      <label>Correo</label>
      <input id="reg_email" class="in" placeholder="correo@dominio.com"/>

      <label>Contrasena</label>
      <input id="reg_pass" type="password" class="in" placeholder="********"/>

      <label>Cargo</label>
      <select id="reg_charge" class="in" onchange="showCamposRol()">
        <option value="">Seleccione cargo...</option>
        <option value="operador">Operador</option>
        <option value="jefe">Jefe</option>
      </select>

      <div id="campos_jefe" class="role-fields">
        <label>Nombre de empresa</label>
        <input id="reg_empresa" class="in" placeholder="Nombre de su empresa"/>
        <label>Crear codigo de empresa</label>
        <input id="reg_codigo" class="in" placeholder="Codigo que usaran sus operadores"/>
      </div>

      <div id="campos_operador" class="role-fields">
        <label>Codigo de empresa</label>
        <input id="reg_codigo_empresa" class="in" placeholder="Codigo proporcionado por su jefe"/>
      </div>

      <button class="btn mt-12" onclick="registerUser()">Registrarme</button>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>

<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const tokenKey = 'sicher.token';
const emailKey = 'sicher.email';
const apiUrl = path => (window.sicherApiUrl ? window.sicherApiUrl(path) : path);

const remembered = localStorage.getItem(emailKey);
if (remembered) {
  $('#email').value = remembered;
  $('#remember').checked = true;
}

function toggleRegistro() {
  const box = $('#regBox');
  box.style.display = (box.style.display === 'block') ? 'none' : 'block';
}

function showCamposRol() {
  const cargo = $('#reg_charge').value;
  $('#campos_jefe').style.display = cargo === 'jefe' ? 'block' : 'none';
  $('#campos_operador').style.display = cargo === 'operador' ? 'block' : 'none';
}

async function registerUser() {
  const cargo = $('#reg_charge').value;
  const payload = {
    nombre: $('#reg_nombre').value.trim(),
    apellido: $('#reg_apellido').value.trim(),
    email: $('#reg_email').value.trim(),
    password: $('#reg_pass').value.trim(),
    charge: cargo,
    codigo: cargo === 'jefe' ? $('#reg_codigo').value.trim() : $('#reg_codigo_empresa').value.trim(),
    empresa_nombre: cargo === 'jefe' ? $('#reg_empresa').value.trim() : undefined
  };

  const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/register'), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  const data = await res.json();
  const err = $('#regErr');

  if (!res.ok) {
    err.style.display = 'block';
    err.textContent = data.error || 'Error al registrar.';
    return;
  }

  err.style.display = 'none';
  alert('Registro exitoso. Ahora inicia sesion.');
  toggleRegistro();
}

async function doLogin() {
  const btn = $('#btn');
  const errBox = $('#err');

  const email = $('#email').value.trim();
  const password = $('#password').value;

  btn.disabled = true;
  errBox.style.display = 'none';

  try {
    const res = await fetch(apiUrl('/login'), {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
    });

    const data = await res.json();

    if (res.status === 403 && data.blocked) {
      errBox.textContent = data.error;
      errBox.style.display = 'block';
      if (data.blocked_until) {
        localStorage.setItem('blocked_until', data.blocked_until);
      }
      window.location.href = '/login_fail';
      return;
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      errBox.textContent = data.error || 'Credenciales invalidas.';
      errBox.style.display = 'block';
      btn.disabled = false;
      return;
    }

    if ($('#remember').checked) {
      localStorage.setItem(emailKey, email);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(emailKey);
    }

    const role = data.role || data.charge || 'operador';

    localStorage.setItem(tokenKey, data.access_token);
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.user_id', String(data.user_id || ''));
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.email', data.email);
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.nombre', data.nombre);
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.apellido', data.apellido);
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.role', role);
    localStorage.setItem('sicher.charge', role);

    if (role === 'jefe') {
      window.location.href = '/admin';
    } else {
      window.location.href = '/dashboard';
    }
  } catch (e) {
    errBox.textContent = 'Error de conexion.';
    errBox.style.display = 'block';
  } finally {
    btn.disabled = false;
  }
}

$('#btn').onclick = doLogin;
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key === 'Enter') doLogin();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

Archivo: app/static/login_fail.html
================================================================================

<!doctype html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8"/>
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"/>
<title>Cuenta bloqueada</title>
<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/login_fail.css"/>
<script src="/config.js"></script>
</head>
<body>
<div class="wrap">
  <div class="shell">
    <h1>Cuenta bloqueada temporalmente</h1>
    <p>Por seguridad, se ha superado el número máximo de intentos de inicio de sesión.</p>
    <p>Podrás volver a intentar en:</p>
    <div id="timer" class="timer">05:00</div>
    <p>Por favor, espera o contacta al administrador si el problema persiste.</p>
    <button id="btn" class="btn" disabled>Volver al inicio de sesión</button>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
const btn = document.getElementById('btn');
const timerEl = document.getElementById('timer');

let remaining = 5 * 60;

const blockedUntil = localStorage.getItem('blocked_until');
if (blockedUntil) {
  const end = new Date(blockedUntil);
  const diff = Math.floor((end - new Date()) / 1000);
  if (diff > 0) remaining = diff;
}

function formatTime(s){
  const m = String(Math.floor(s/60)).padStart(2,'0');
  const sec = String(s%60).padStart(2,'0');
  return `${m}:${sec}`;
}

function updateTimer(){
  timerEl.textContent = formatTime(remaining);
  if(remaining <= 0){
    clearInterval(interval);
    btn.disabled = false;
    btn.textContent = "Reintentar inicio de sesión";
  } else remaining--;
}

const interval = setInterval(updateTimer, 1000);
updateTimer();

btn.onclick = () => {
  localStorage.removeItem('blocked_until');
  window.location.href = '/';
};
</script>
</body>
</html>
